Gate: `proof.mjs standing` 274/274 receipts, world-guard ok, 11_standing 5/5; check-versions 274; duplication 11/11; tsc/eslint/build clean; rpc.stats 7/7 PASS (the new 1d/1w test); the probe answers are byte-identical without and with the indexes and the plans name each index (1d 6 ms / 1w 2 ms on the probe world). **Deeper cause (owner 10-31: *"DO NOT CHANGE ANYTHING REGARDING TIME - THE SECONDLY DATA RENEWAL STAYS IN STATS"*):** the every-minute market (live 10-30 ~00:35) raised the lap-close rate from ~669-1,635/h to ~2,505-3,220/h, and the drift rewrites ~139k/154,734 `port_goods` rows a minute (718.8M updates, 362.2M HOT); the count already averaged 51.2 s before its first 120.2 s timeout. The 0284 ledger-points trigger is not the driver (3 ledger inserts/s). **0287 adds indexes only — no cron, schedule, TTL, knob, client cadence or function body.** Dev-log `docs/dev-log/2026-10-31-stats-timeout.md` (before/after, PROD CHECKS, the after-check and the one-time fold command if the cron's 2 min cannot carry the backlog).

- 2026-10-30 **0283 a hull starts at her rolled max — PR #405 open, CI running** (the bug behind today's `rpc.surface` red): `public.commission_ship` (0276) stored a new hull's durability as `class × roll` UNFLOORED while `public.ship_base` — her max — returns `floor(class × roll_hull)`, so a hull whose roll product is fractional started ABOVE her own maximum (CI 405.6 vs 405, 469.2 vs 469; seed-dependent, hence two reds and one green today). Prod 2026-10-30 (read-only, head 0282): 917 ships, every `roll_hull` 1.000, 0 above max — latent, fixed for the next fractional roll. ONE substitution in that one body; body = pre-image + that substitution (md5 43640ad5… → ffcd4108…), ACL/volatility/definer/config unmoved, every other function unmoved (661); rolled-back probe: a barca rolled 1.043, product 417.200, commissioned through the real door → stored 417.00 = ship_base's 417. - 2026-10-29 **0273 a sale is the whole stack, BUILT** (`osn-sell-crumbs`; PR #389 open, not deployed). Root = prod's (read-only, head 0272): the two stuck merchants' whole holds are sub-ton remnants (0.02-0.96 t), and `cmd.resolve_qty` (0007:296-325, never re-cut) floors every sale to `floor(qty)` = 0, so 0272's cut-loss SELLs are all skipped `E_NO_CARGO` and the loop stays open — fleet-wide, 62,600 skipped SELLs / 6 h across 828 fleets (the normal `good_id null, qty null, at_profit true` line expands to one `SELL <good> ALL` per sub-ton stack). Fix: re-cut `public.cmd.resolve_qty` ONLY — a SALE is the exact tonnage she carries, a PURCHASE stays in whole tuns: the mode resolves on the raw `v_max` (`HALF` → `v_max/2`, `PCT` → `v_max × pct / 100`) and `return case when p_side = 'sell' then v_qty else floor(v_qty) end`. Proven: a buy is identical for every `v_max` and mode; a sell is identical exactly when its resolved quantity is a whole tun (3.4 t ALL now sells 3.4, not 3 + a stranded crumb; HALF of 3 sells 1.5). Nothing else moves. Local (cached gate): 261 restored + 0273 applied, receipt green — hunks reverse to prod's own body (md5 999e9f6b…, tail pinned), ACL/volatility/definer/config unmoved, every other function in the four schemas unmoved (md5), 44-point arithmetic grid; direct probes 0.93 ALL/HALF/PCT 30 (old E_NO_CARGO / new exact tonnage, stock restored, purse credited), 3.4 (old 3 + crumb / new 3.4), whole 4 t identical, empty hold, BUY ALL/HALF/PCT identical; the queue (render → parse → enqueue → advance: old skips E_NO_CARGO, new executes + credits; bid below basis still refuses, whole 2 t identical rows); the seam (20 d. hire, hold 1799 d. in 2 stacks): OLD loops (SELL E_NO_CARGO, HIRE E_INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS, SAIL E_CREW_SHORT), NEW closes (sells at the bid, HIRE paid, SAIL departs, crew restored, purse 367 d., ledger reconciled). `proof.mjs standing` 5/5, `merchant` 6/6. **Known CI trap:** a merge made with a session token triggers NO workflow — no Acceptance, no Pages
deploy. Dispatch `deploy-pages.yml` and `acceptance.yml` by hand after any such merge (never
`migrations-apply-proof.yml`: a dispatch sets FROM_ZERO there and rebuilds the world).

**Reading a red CI run from a cloud container:** the job log and the Playwright report are on a
blob host the network policy answers 403 to. The `acceptance` VERDICT job's annotations are
readable and name the shard and the count:
`gh api repos/gkwngns714-spec/byeharu-voyage/check-runs/<id>/annotations`.

**EXACT DEPLOY PROCEDURE (any PC that has done `docs/NEW_COMPUTER.md`):**
1. `cd ~/byeharu-voyage && git fetch && gh pr list` → read each PR's latest comment from its cloud routine; `gh pr checks <n>`; required = `build` + `disposable-chain` green.
2. Prod head: `npx --yes supabase@2.120.0 db query --linked "select max(version) from supabase_migrations.schema_migrations"` (must be the number just below the PR's migration).
3. Run the PR's dev-log "PROD CHECKS FOR THE MAIN SESSION" blocks read-only (`db query --linked -f <file>`); every row as expected. If one fails, do not push: send it back to a cloud routine.
4. `~/bin/bv-deploy.sh <pr>`: it unwinds the clock, pushes, re-winds, and merges ONLY if `push_rc=0` and head = the PR's migration. On `NOT MERGING`, read `~/bv-push-logs/push-<version>.log` to its last error; never just re-run.
5. After it lands: check the clock (`select count(*) filter (where active) from cron.job where jobname like 'byeharu-voyage:%'` = 8), the Pages deploy (`gh run list --workflow deploy-pages.yml -L 1`), then update this file and `~/bin/bv-queue-push.sh`.

**FROM THE CLOUD (claude.ai/code, repo gkwngns714-spec/byeharu-voyage):** code, tests, PRs work today. **Deploys can't run from the cloud yet:** its environment has no `SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN` (probe `trig_01Y4kboFF5vp35XEDAR7h9Bi`, 08:34: "not set"). One-time unblock (owner): supabase.com/dashboard/account/tokens → new token `claude-cloud`; claude.ai/code → environment selector → this environment's settings → Environment variables `SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN=sbp_…`; Network access **Custom**: `api.supabase.com`, `supabase.co`, `*.supabase.co`, `supabase.com`, `*.supabase.com` + "include default package managers". Then re-run the probe; if it reads head 0213+, a cloud session can follow the procedure above with `bash scripts/ops/bv-deploy.sh <pr>` (repo path differs: edit `~/byeharu-voyage` in the script to the clone's path). Unverified: whether the DB connection (ports 5432/6543) passes the cloud proxy.

**Cloud lessons (10-20):** routines created via the API did not show in the owner's claude.ai list; follow them on GitHub (branches and PR comments). A cloud sandbox builds the PGlite world from zero (~2.5 h): every routine brief says *no from-zero world build; tsc + eslint + build + pure specs; CI proves the chain*.
